in astronomy and navigation, sight of a thing or a signal)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They recovered three of the explorers very much alive, then another, wracked with severe hypothermia, who was taken to hospital.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To salvage, to extricate, to rescue (a thing or person)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"At the top of the hill I asked to stop for a few minutes to recover my strength.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To replenish to, resume (a good state of mind or body)."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The plaintiff recovered in his suit, being awarded declaratory relief and a clearing of his name.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To obtain a positive judgement;
